## 2) The Duplicate List (Conflict Zones)

Semantic duplicates are detected by matching leaf key names across `trading.yaml` and `domains.yaml` within a focus token set.
This is intended to spotlight shadow configs after refactors.

**Trading-side candidates**
- `trading.risk.score_weights.absorption_inverse` — **UNUSED**

**Domains-side candidates**
- `domains.risk_management.risk_score_weights.absorption_inverse` — **UNUSED**

## Schema vs YAML Reconciliation

### Loader Migrations / Structure Drift (By Design)

The loader performs explicit migrations before Pydantic validation:
- `system.yaml` root meta keys (e.g. `sequential_tests`, `risk_core`, `kelly`, …) are moved under `system_meta.*`.
- `regime.yaml:config_version` is moved to `system_meta.regime_config_version`.
- `regime.yaml:hotreload_whitelist` stays at root and is also copied into `system_meta.hotreload_whitelist`.
- `domains.yaml` is loaded under `domains.*` (entire file is namespaced).
- `strategies.yaml` is loaded under `strategies_registry.*`.
- `strategies/<id>.yaml` is loaded under `strategies.<id>.*` (with optional top-level `<id>` wrapper).

### YAML Keys Not Representable by Schema (Unloadable Data)

(none detected after normalization)

### Code → Schema Drift (Potential Runtime Crashes)

These config paths are referenced in code but do not exist in `apps/reference/config_models.py`.
If the referenced module is executed, this is likely an `AttributeError` / fail-closed crash.

- `orders` (in: apps\reference\domains\execution_position\fsm.py)
- `strategies.aurora.assets.*.leverage.mode.lower` (in: apps\reference\domains\execution_position\bootstrapping\leverage_bootstrapper.py)
- `strategies.mean_reversion.assets.*.leverage.mode.lower` (in: apps\reference\domains\execution_position\bootstrapping\leverage_bootstrapper.py)
- `trading.instruments` (in: apps\reference\domains\execution_position\config_resolver.py)
- `trading.orders` (in: apps\reference\domains\execution_position\fsm.py)
- `trading.watchdog` (in: apps\reference\domains\execution_position\fsm.py)
- `trading.watchdog.ack_ttl_ms` (in: apps\reference\domains\execution_position\fsm.py)
